# The code-cut map (#430) — every legacy site that must cut over to the cascade

> **Status:** plan / master audit artifact · **Kind:** PURE AUDIT (no code written to produce it).
> **Authority it executes:** [cutover.md](cutover.md) §0 (this IS the "comprehensive code-cut map" that doc mandates),
> [cascade-engine-430.md](cascade-engine-430.md) §4 (the demolition map this extends to the full surface),
> [validation.md](../../specs/validation.md) §1 (shadow-then-cut). Per-channel spec authority is cited inline.

## What this is

For **every** legacy Info-variable-driven mechanism and every consumer that reads legacy classification, this map records:
the **legacy mechanism** (grounded in live `file:line`), the **cascade replacement**, and the **cut action**
(`delete` / `rewire` / `bridge` / `keep` / `verify` / `BLOCKED`). It is the line-item plan the cutover works down, and
simultaneously the **Gate 1 completeness proof**: a StoneBase/spec-mapped source with **no cut site** (or a `NONE FOUND`
replacement on a non-deferred site) is exactly the game-breaking gap Gate 1 hunts — those are surfaced at the top.

## How it was built (and its limits)

Two-pass, adversarial, per [DEC-all-means-all](../../architecture/decisions.md#dec-all-means-all): a **finder** mapped
each channel from the specs + live code, then an independent **adversarial critic** assumed the finder was incomplete and
hunted misses (a careful solo pass once missed 77 sources). **13 channels** across three axes — **DELETE** (legacy
accumulators/gates/apply-loops/caches), **REWIRE** (legacy-classification consumers), **PREREQ** (self-containment reads,
grants apply-loop, blocked data tail).

- **425** cut-sites total: **306** mapped by finders + **119** added by the adversarial pass.
- Of those: **6** genuine Gate-1/prereq gaps (DELETE/PREREQ, no replacement) · **45** Gate-3 consumption items (REWIRE, not-yet-wired) · **93** deferred/BLOCKED by ruling.
- **Pass 2 (fresh adversarial re-derivation + cross-cutting critics):** +**20** cut-sites Pass 1 missed, **8** Pass-1 corrections, **12** reclassifications, and **6** NEW whole-subsystem Gate-1 gaps the per-channel fan-out structurally could not see (vision, air/nuke, great-people, property, minted-gold …). See §Pass 2.
- **38** finder⇄critic corrections (doc-vs-code / resolver-gap divergences, with proof) — §Divergences.
- **55** unresolved questions needing an owner ruling — §Unresolved.
- ⚠ **No channel self-certified complete** — every critic still found something. This is a FIRST adversarial pass; a
  second (fresh re-derivation) is planned. Treat every entry as grounded-but-not-yet-exhausted; the `file:line` anchors
  **drift** — trust the named function, re-confirm the line ([DEC-no-guessing](../../architecture/decisions.md#dec-no-guessing)).

**Legend:** `[map]` finder-mapped · `🔎[adv]` adversarial-pass find · a cut-site is grounded in BOTH its spec doc and a
live `file:line`. `NONE FOUND` = no cascade replacement exists yet (Gate-1 risk unless the row is `BLOCKED`/deferred).

## ⚖ Rulings addendum (owner, 2026-07-02) — how the rulings land on this map's rows

Authoritative text: [cutover.md](cutover.md) §Owner rulings 2026-07-02. Row-level effect:

| Ruling | Affected rows | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| **All unported modifier channels are PRE-CUTOVER** | BLOCKED rows 2–11 (maintenance / defense / health / happiness / GP-trade-air scalars / buildRate) + unresolved #10 | The "NOT BUILT" blockers become **in-#430 build items**: each channel gets spec → StoneBase parity → C++ port → shadow → cut. Rows stay BLOCKED *until its machine exists*, but are scoped work, not deferrals. |
| **GP-rate BASE economy + building lineOfSight → cascade home** | Pass-2 Gate-1 gaps #1–2 (+ the air-unit-capacity / power-accumulator minors ride with their banks) | Modelled in the cascade pre-cutover; mechanism pinned in the spec work when the owning channel is built. No BLOCKED-deferral rows. |
| **Capabilities: dedicated walkthrough PENDING** | Gate-1 (Pass-2) #5–6, Gate-3/BLOCKED capability items 19–20/89–90, unresolved #35–40 | Frozen until the walkthrough; nothing in the capability channel cuts. |
| **Self-containment: dedicated walkthrough PENDING** | PREREQ rows 80–86, unresolved #45–50 | Frozen until the walkthrough. (The corporationRevenue derive-from-held-techs fix proceeds regardless — StoneBase-fidelity, PREREQ item 2.) |
| **Tally counts are KEEP, not gaps** | Gate-1 rows 4–6, unresolved #17–18 | `countNumBuildings` (cities-having) + `CvTeam::getHasReligionCount`/`getHasCorporationCount` stay engine-owned object counts (the deliberate read-not-store design — don't rip up to replace with same); tally accessor added only when a cascade consumer needs it. Reclassify verify→**KEEP**. |
| **freeSpecialists are MODIFIERS, never grants** | Unresolved #75 (BLOCKED grants row), grants-machine inventory's freeSpecialist-shaped entries | The `changeFreeSpecialistCount` applies classify to the modifier plane (alive only while the source is — building present / civic adopted / trait active); reclassify out of the grants channel. |

| **The getter-contract cut strategy** (2026-07-02) | Unresolved #8, PREREQ rows 80–86, unresolved #45–50 | Getters are the stable contracts: instrument each with an in-body event-spine "cascadeValue" emit (shadow rides real consumer calls), flip the body to the cascade at clean parity, delete the accumulator behind it — **consumers never rewire**. The self-containment reads are thereby fine (each read target is itself cascade-backed after its flip); raw-saved-state getters (isGoldenAge / isDisorder / trait membership) never flip. Flip in dependency order. Full text: [cutover.md](cutover.md) §Rulings item 5. |

## ⛔ Gate 1 / prerequisite gaps — DELETE/PREREQ sources with NO cascade replacement (non-deferred)

The genuine game-breakers: a live **DELETE-axis** (modifier/enabler/tally) or **PREREQ-axis** (self-containment /
grants-resolver) mechanism whose replacement is **`NONE FOUND`** and is **not** deferred. When legacy is cut, this
contribution silently vanishes — each must gain a cascade replacement (or be reclassified) **before its cut**.
_(REWIRE-axis `NONE FOUND`s are the separate Gate-3 worklist below — those are expected-unwired, not completeness gaps.)_

| # | Channel | Legacy mechanism (no replacement) | Cut | Location(s) | Replacement text |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

not yet consumed** by the engine — the `NONE FOUND` is the concrete queryable not existing yet. This is cutover.md
Gate 3 (the "biggest, largely-parallel piece / long pole"), **expected work, not a completeness gap**. ⚠ Some of these
may instead be a legacy flag with **no home in any block** (a true data gap) — the per-site notes in the detail below
disambiguate; Pass 2 (fresh re-derivation) sharpens this split.

## ⏳ BLOCKED — deferred by ruling (NOT Gate 1 gaps)

Recorded so they are never mistaken for a Gate-1 gap. Each stays legacy past the first cut; its blocker is named.

| # | Channel | Legacy mechanism | Blocked by |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | DELETE: enabler gates + caches | CvPlayer::canFound (found-a-city rule: distance/area/water/peak validity, capability half) | canFound founding RULE (distance/area/water/peak) DEFERRED (owner ruling 2026-06-30, cascade-engine-430.md §7.4) — engine logic 'a bit all over the place', not cleanly spec'd; a look-at-later gate not modelled now |
| 2 | DELETE: modifier city accumulators + process loops | 🔎m_iMaintenance (mutable cache) + m_iMaintenanceModifier -- city maintenance accumulators (sec5) | maintenance cascade machine (cost-asymmetric getModifiedIntValue combiner) NOT BUILT |
| 3 | DELETE: modifier city accumulators + process loops | 🔎m_iBuildingDefense / m_iBuildingBombardDefense / m_iEspionageDefenseModifier -- city defense + espionage-defense accumulators (sec4) | defense cascade machine NOT BUILT |
| 4 | DELETE: modifier city accumulators + process loops | 🔎m_iBuildingGoodHealth / m_iBuildingBadHealth / m_iExtraBuildingGoodHealth / m_iExtraBuildingBadHealth -- building health accumulators (sec3 goodHealth/badHealth) | health cascade machine (signed-split good/bad polarity) NOT BUILT |
| 5 | DELETE: modifier city accumulators + process loops | GreatPeople + trade + air/misc city modifier scalars — m_iGreatPeopleRateModifier, m_iTradeRouteModifier, m_iForeignTradeRouteModifier, m_iExtraTradeRoutes, m_iAirModifier, m_iWarWearinessModifier, m_iHurryAngerModifier | GATE-1 GAP: these are additional modifier channels (GP-rate, trade routes, war-weariness) with no cascade home. modifier.md §6 names greatPeopleRate as a family conceptually but no calc implements it. |
| 6 | DELETE: modifier city accumulators + process loops | 🔎m_iExtraBuildingGoodHappiness / m_iExtraBuildingBadHappiness (+ building happiness accumulators) -- sec3 happyLevel/unhappyLevel | happiness cascade machine (signed-split + ~22 anger sources) NOT BUILT |
| 7 | DELETE: modifier city accumulators + process loops | The DEFENSE accumulator bank — m_iBuildingDefense, m_iBuildingBombardDefense, m_iMinimumDefenseLevel, m_iExtraMinDefense, m_iExtraLocalDynamicDefense, m_iExtraRiverDefensePenalty, m_iExtraBuildingDefenseRecoverySpeedModifier, m_iModifiedBuildingDefenseRecoverySpeedCap, m_iExtraCityDefenseRecoverySpeedModifier, m_iExtraLocalCaptureProbability/ResistanceModifier, m_iEspionageDefenseModifier, m_iAirModifier, m_iNukeModifier, m_bonusDefenseChanges | GATE-1 GAP: the defense channel (modifier.md §2 min member floors defense) is UNPORTED — no calc implements the min-combine. |

| 37 | PREREQ: grants apply-loop + blocked data tail | CvCascadeGrants.cpp gr_resolveBuilding (:114-128) | RESOLVER GAP: building FreeSpecialTech curates to grants.techs (a LIST bucket — curate_building.py:594-596 grants.setdefault('techs').append(st)) but gr_resolveBuilding reads only freeTechs (a pulse), freePromotions, repeatable, goldenAge, population — never the 'techs' list. So the building bFirst FreeSpecialTech grant (CvCity::setupBuilding:14757) is un-resolved by the cascade despite the data being mapped. |
| 38 | PREREQ: grants apply-loop + blocked data tail | grants-machine.md inventory row 23 (religion) + finder note | Finder correctly flagged that the free-unit apply is in foundReligion not setHolyCity; consistent with my read. No correction to the finder there, but the inventory row-23 label 'CvPlayer::setHolyCity block' in grants-machine.md is imprecise for the same reason (real site: the foundReligion award block) — a doc-line to tighten in the same pass per fix-docs-now. |

# Pass 2 — adversarial re-derivation & cross-cutting audit

A second, independent pass ([DEC-all-means-all](../../architecture/decisions.md#dec-all-means-all)): each channel was
**re-derived from scratch** off the specs + live code (not trusting Pass 1) and diffed against the Pass 1 slice, and
**cross-cutting critics** attacked the whole map for gaps the per-channel fan-out is structurally blind to.

## ⛔ Pass 2 — NEW whole-subsystem Gate-1 gaps (cross-cutting)

Legacy families / StoneBase-modelled sources that **no channel owned** — the highest-value kraken finds (a whole
subsystem silently vanishing at the cut). Each grounded in `file:line` + StoneBase where relevant.

| # | Area (uncovered subsystem / source) | Legacy family | Should be owned by | Example location(s) | Proof |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Great People rate BASE economy — the INPUT side of GP generation | m_iBaseGreatPeopleRate + m_paiGreatPeopleUnitRate + m_iGreatPeopleProgress / m_paiGreatPeopleUnitProgress (CvCity), driven by CvBuildingInfo::getGreatPeopleRateChange() and CvSpecialistInfo::getGreatPeopleRateChange() | DELETE: modifier city accumulators + process loops (as a new modifier channel), or a dedicated GP-economy channel | `C:\code\s2s\s2s\Sources\Engine\CvCity.h:1546 (int m_iBaseGreatPeopleRate)`<br>`C:\code\s2s\s2s\Sources\Engine\CvCity.cpp:5104 (processBuilding: changeBaseGreatPeopleRate(kBuilding.getGreatPeopleRateChange()*iChange))`<br>`C:\code\s2s\s2s\Sources\Engine\CvCity.cpp:5167 (processSpecialist: changeBaseGreatPeopleRate from CvSpecialistInfo::getGreatPeopleRateChange())`<br>`C:\code\s2s\s2s\Sources\Engine\CvCity.cpp:5110 / :5164 (changeGreatPeopleUnitRate — which GP unit is born)`<br>`C:\code\s2s\s2s\Sources\Engine\CvCity.cpp:7186 (changeBaseGreatPeopleRate); :7170 (getGreatPeopleRate applies modifier to base)`<br>`C:\code\s2s\s2s\Sources\Engine\CvCity.h:1805 (m_paiGreatPeopleUnitProgress)` | The code-cut-map enumerates ONLY the GP-rate MODIFIER (m_iGreatPeopleRateModifier) — listed as a BLOCKED Gate-1 scalar at code-cut-map.md:119/641/642 ('modifier.md §6 names greatPeopleRate as a family conceptually but no calc implements it'). Grepping the code-cut-map for 'm_iBaseGreatPeopleRate\|changeBaseGreatPeopleRate\|getBaseGreatPeopleRate\|getGreatPeopleRateChange\|GreatPeopleUnitRate\|m_iGreatPeopleProgress' returns ZERO hits. Yet these are live Info-variable-driven accumulators: CvBuildingInfo::getGreatPeopleRateChange() and CvSpecialistInfo::getGreatPeopleRateChange() feed changeBaseGreatPeopleRate() in processBuilding (CvCity.cpp:5104) and processSpecialist (CvCity.cpp:5167). When those process-loops are cut, the GP-point BASE (m_iBaseGreatPeopleRate) and the which-unit-gets-born tracking (m_paiGreatPeopleUnitRate/Progress) silently vanish — the MODIFIER having a BLOCKED home does not cover the BASE the modifier multiplies. No spec (modifier.md has zero 'greatPeople' hits) defines the GP base-rate calc; only json.md:429 mentions a deferred 'greatPeople' data block. This is a whole mechanism family with no cut site. |

If the curator does not emit it for that grantor, or the parse drops it, or the shape cannot carry the legacy flag's parameterization, then no block covers the flag for that source → a true Gate-1 DATA gap that must surface as game-breaking. Headline: the building-attributes block (17 flags, items 21-43) is CORRECTLY Gate-3 (emitted + block exists, only governmentCenter consumed). But the civic-policy items 44-45 are MISPLACED in the Gate-3 table — the map's own detail entries (lines 2127/2132/2149) correctly call them Gate-1 gaps, contradicting the summary table. Several capability items are worse-than-Gate-3 too._

These 22 are the confirmedSolid count. === CORRECTIONS RE-VERIFIED (both right) === (1) changeExtraBuildType (28865): body constructs/mutates m_worker (UnitCompWorker::setExtraBuild), NOT a `+= iChange` magnitude — Pass 1 correctly classes it as skill/enabler-plane membership toggle, not a strength accumulator. #### PREREQ: grants apply-loop + blocked data tail

_Re-confirmed 18 Pass-1 sites._

**Missed by Pass 1 (new cut-sites):**
1. BLOCKED — grants apply-loop (increment 5) not built AND gr_resolveBuilding has no 'buildings' grant channel; needs a resolver extension before the cut, else building-granted free buildings silently vanish at cut (a Gate-1 grants gap). — Building getFreeBuilding / getFreeAreaBuilding — an active building GRANTS another free building to the empire (getFreeBuilding) or to every city in its area (getFreeAreaBuilding). Applied (reversibly, keyed on iChange) in CvPlayer::processBuilding, and physically REALIZED into cities at CvCity::init (getFreeBuildingCount>0 -> setFreeBuilding; getFreeAreaBuildingCount -> changeFreeAreaBuildingCount). This is a genuine building->buildings grant handout distinct from the settler foundBuildings and the game-start civ buildings.
    - loc: `Sources/Engine/CvPlayer.cpp CvPlayer::processBuilding :7367-7378 (getFreeBuilding -> changeFreeBuildingCount ; getFreeAreaBuilding -> per-area changeFreeAreaBuildingCount)`<br>`Sources/Engine/CvCity.cpp CvCity::init :367-375 (getFreeAreaBuildingCount>0 -> changeFreeAreaBuildingCount ; else getFreeBuildingCount>0 -> setFreeBuilding — the realization site)`<br>`Sources/Engine/CvPlayer.cpp getFreeBuildingCount :13435 (accumulator accessor)`
    - → **replace:** ⛔ NONE FOUND in the grants machine. gr_resolveBuilding (CvCascadeGrants.cpp:114-127) reads ONLY freePromotions/freeTechs/population/repeatable/goldenAge — it never reads a grants.buildings list on a building, so a building-granted free building is un-resolved by the cascade. On the curator side FreeBuilding/FreeAreaBuilding are STORE_TAGS (curate_building.py:231) treated as enabler edges that 'invert onto the SOURCE' (availability/enables), NOT a grants.buildings apply — so the APPLY (physically seating the free building) has no cascade resolver.
    - proof: Ground truth: read Sources/Engine/CvPlayer.cpp:7358-7383 (processBuilding: getFreeBuilding -> changeFreeBuildingCount, getFreeAreaBuilding -> per-area loop) and Sources/Engine/CvCity.cpp:365-376 (init: getFreeBuildingCount>0 -> setFreeBuilding). Grep of CvCascadeGrants.cpp gr_resolveBuilding (lines 114-127) shows reads of freePromotions/freeTechs/population/repeatable/goldenAge only — no 'buildings' list. curate_building.py:231 shows FreeBuilding/FreeAreaBuilding are STORE_TAGS ('DROP building-side: they invert onto the SOURCE'), i.e. modeled as enables edges, not a grants apply. ABSENT from ch_12.json: neither cutSites nor missedSites references getFreeBuilding/getFreeAreaBuilding/changeFreeBuildingCount/setFreeBuilding — searched all 27 entries; the only 'foundBuildings' (settler) and civ game-start buildings are covered, this building->building grant is not.

**Reclassifications:**
- Civic revolution grant (getRevIdxSwitchTo pulse on civic adopt) — cutSites entry, cutAction=BLOCKED: **apply-loop / BLOCKED on the grants apply-loop (increment 5)** → **REWIRE (Python-consumer), effectively a NONE-in-DLL cut — there is NO DLL apply-loop to build; the grants apply-loop (increment 5) does NOT own this** — Pass 1's OWN notes and uncertainties correctly establish getRevIdxSwitchTo has no DLL apply site (only CyInfoInterface1 export + CvGameTextMgr UI help; application is RevolutionDCM Python), yet it still tags the cutAction as BLOCKED-on-the-grants-apply-loop and lists it as an apply-loop kind. Ground truth (grants-machine.md row: 'RevolutionDCM Python (no DLL apply)' + my grep confirming no DLL apply) means the grants apply-loop increment 5 has nothing to apply here — the cut is a Python rewire outside the DLL demolition map. Minor: the classification/blockedBy is internally inconsistent with Pass 1's own verified note, not a factual error about the code.
- unitcombat->tags (mechanized/gunpowder/mounted) — cutSites entry, kind='other', blockedBy=curate_unitcombat.py emits no tags: **other / grants-channel cut-site** → **Correctly a Gate-3 classification DATA gap (json §8 tags), NOT a grants apply-loop or blocked-data-tail-for-grants item — it belongs to the tags-consumption channel, included here only as adjacent context** — This is not a grants apply-loop mechanism at all; it is unit tags classification consumption (Gate 3). Pass 1 rightly flags it BLOCKED on the unitcombat->tag curator pass, but placing it in the grants apply-loop channel is a scope stretch. Not a correctness error — the blockedBy is accurate — but a channel-attribution note: it is the tags consumer's cut, carried here as part of the 'blocked data tail' the prompt names. Recorded so the map does not double-count it as a grants site.

_notes:_ Pass 1's ch_12.json is exceptionally thorough: beyond its cutSites it carries a self-audit missedSites block that ALREADY caught the 5 game-start apply-loop sites (CvPlayer::initFreeState startingGold+speed-scaler, initFreeUnits starting-units, CvGame::initFreeState civ free-techs+pre-startEra, CvCity::init civ free-buildings + era freePopulation, CvPlayer::initMore/resetCivTypeEffects initial civics) PLUS the resolver gaps (gr_resolvePlayerInit reads only civics/techs/buildings/startingGold — I independently re-confirmed at CvCascadeGrants.cpp:185-199; starting-units + freePopulation are DATA-mapped but resolver-silent) PLUS building bFirst FreeSpecialTech (grants.techs list, resolver-blind) and globalPopulation (empire pop pulse) PLUS the civic free-specialist (:18118) classification uncertainty. I independently re-confirmed ALL of these against ground truth (file:line reads listed in confirmedSolid). Because they live in Pass 1's own missedSites, they are NOT 'missed by Pass 1' per the strict schema meaning ('the slice lacks') — the slice-as-a-whole has them; I did not duplicate them into missedByPass1. The ONE genuine grant apply-loop absent from BOTH Pass 1 blocks is building getFreeBuilding/getFreeAreaBuilding (processBuilding:7367 -> realized CvCity::init:372) — a building that hands the empire/area another free building, with no cascade resolver (gr_resolveBuilding reads no 'buildings' list) and curated as a STORE_TAG enables-edge, not a grants apply. All other Pass 1 cutSites (unit promotions, settler foundBuildings, doPropertyUnitSpawn, doHeal, building freePromotions/bFirst grants, trait freePromotions/eraAdvanceFreeSpecialist/GP-birth-goldenAge, tech first-discover, religion foundReligion:8848 [Pass 1 correctly notes grants-machine.md's 'setHolyCity block' label is imprecise — the real award site is foundReligion:8848, gated on bAward], civic revolution, game-start), and the deferred tail (paralyze/state, NPC stronglyRestricted, corp-system, ranked-target largestCity, missions/CvOutcome, revolution, leaderhead-remap) I independently re-confirmed against live code — 0 factually-wrong location/replacement/cutAction claims found (wrongInPass1 empty). Two misclassification NOTES (not errors): the civic revolution cut is a Python rewire not a DLL apply-loop cut, and unitcombat->tags is a Gate-3 tags-consumption item carried here as adjacent 'blocked data tail' context. #### DELETE: enabler gates + caches

_Coverage:_ Method: read enabler.md, cutover.md, and cascade-engine-430.md §4 in full first, then traced every gate named in the channel scope in live code (grounded each in file:line by function name). Enumerated exhaustively (locust): every legacy availability gate (CvCity/CvPlayer canConstruct+Internal, canTrain(UnitTypes)+Internal+the UnitCombat overload, canCreate, canMaintain; CvPlayer canEverResearch/canResearch/canDoCivics/canFoundReligion/canFound; CvGame canEverResearch); every cache (CvCity m_bCanConstruct map + flush/note, CvCity canTrain cache under CAN_TRAIN_CACHING + upgrade cache, CvPlayer's FOUR m_bCanConstruct* arrays + two clear methods + alloc/free, setBuildingListInvalid, VALIDATE_BUILDING_CACHE_CONSISTENCY); the CABV PreLoop + body; the #195 ConstructRequirement/getBuildingsEnabledBy index (marked keep-as-enables-seed / delete-fidelity-scaffolding per §4); setHasBuilding chain-walk. Cross-checked the BUILT cascade replacements by reading Cascade/CvCascadeEnabler.{h,cpp} end-to-end (10+2 gates BUILT + StoneBase-ported). Confirmed StoneBase enabler sources (TechCascade/BuildingCascade/UnitCascade/CivicCascade/BuildCascade/ConditionEvaluator) each have a cascade cut-side representation (Gate 1). Enumerated the consumer surface (81 can* call sites across 18 files): itemized the Python/UI Cy* wrappers and the AI doProduction/CvGameInterface seams as rewire targets pointing at the enabler frontier. Deliberately EXCLUDED from cut-sites: the object-owned counts (getBuildingCount/getUnitCount) which the tally READS (STAYS per §4, not this channel); the modifier accumulators (separate channel); the per-plot CvPlot::canBuild worker gate (kept as the build gate per §7.4, not a delete). Line numbers grounded in the live files (re-verified against the 2026-06-29 demolition map where they had drifted — e.g. CvPlayer cache clears are :28077/:28104 today, not the map's :28056/:28076).

_Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +9 missed, 4 corrections.

#### DELETE: modifier empire (civics/traits/tech)

_Coverage:_ Enumerated exhaustively by: (1) reading the four named apply-loops IN FULL from live code — CvPlayer::processCivics (18005-18256), CvPlayer::processTrait (28463-28716), CvTeam::processTech (5907-6164), CvPlayer::processTech (30923-30952) — plus their DRIVERS setCivics (14297) / recalculateModifiers (28718) and the fan-out tail CvCity::processTech (24038); every change*/update* deposit call read at its line and bucketed into YIELD/COMMERCE (cascade-covered) vs EMPIRE-STATE (no calc) vs CAPABILITY/enabler/score (other channels). (2) Confirmed the cascade replacement scope against the BUILT ports in Sources/Cascade/ (only CvCascadeYieldRate/CommerceCalc/PercentStack/BuildingPackage/YieldBasePackages/ModifierMath/MMKernel — yield+commerce+corporation ONLY) and against StoneBase src/Application/Features/Calc/ (PercentStack.cs reads state.Civics+state.Traits empire.percent; CommercePackages.cs reads state.Techs corporationRevenue; ModifierMath.ActiveTraitSet is the trait active-set model). (3) Confirmed the curator DID map the empire-STATE families into CvJsonInfo (grep Assets/Data/civics: maintenance/greatPeopleRate/tradeRoutes/warWeariness/upkeep/hurry/corporation/health/happiness/... all present as authored families) — so DATA has an InfoRepo home but CALC does not. (4) Verified data-inertness of the tech commerce modifier (0 iCommerceModifier in CIV4TechInfos.xml). Deliberately EXCLUDED from this channel (named to avoid double-ownership, not skipped): the CvInfoReplacements trait-table swap + m_pabHasTrait line resolution (infra channel, cascade-engine-430 §6); the tech CAPABILITY flags (canFoundOnPeaks etc. — enabler/capabilities channel, already shadow-proven via en_empireHasCapability); obsolete-building bookkeeping (enabler channel); TechScore/Assets/Power (out-of-scope demographics per legacy-value-calc-map BLUF); doSetFreePromotions (unit-plane channel). _Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +6 missed, 2 corrections.

<details><summary>adversarial method / proof-of-search</summary>

Assumed the finder incomplete per DEC-all-means-all. Ran independent greps over Sources/Engine/ for the apply-loop drivers named in the channel map itself: CvPlayer::(setCivics|processCivics|processTrait|processTech|recalculateModifiers) and CvTeam::processTech. The finder's civic+trait sites (setCivics 14297, processCivics 18005, processTrait 28463, recalculateModifiers 28718) verified line-accurate. BUT the finder's cutSites JSON contains NO entry for the entire TECH plane despite its own map text explicitly listing CvTeam::processTech and CvPlayer::processTech. Read all three tech apply-loops in full (CvPlayer::processTech 30923, CvTeam::processTech 5907, CvCity::processTech 24038) plus the tech-side reset+reapply driver CvTeam::recalculateModifiers 7675 (re-runs processTech 7769) and the player-modifier reset CvPlayer::clearModifierTotals 28117 (fanned to CvCity::clearModifierTotals). Also found CvPlayer::processHeritage 31029 (a commerce empire-flat deposit, re-run by recalculateModifiers 28845). Cross-checked StoneBase: PercentStack.cs uses ModifierMath.SourcePercent over state.Civics + ActiveTraitSet(state,TraitsSimple,TraitsComplex); ModifierMath.KeyedPlotYield models improvement-keyed plot yields — the target of CvTeam::processTech's changeImprovementYieldChange. Confirmed tech modifier families ARE curated in Assets/Data/techs/ (inflation/maintenance/tradeRoutes present) — same data-mapped/calc-partial shape the finder found for civics. Residual risk: did not exhaustively enumerate every one of the ~30 tech deposit setters into cascade-family names, but the driver-level cut sites are named and grounded.

</details>

#### DELETE: modifier unit extra-stat stack

_Coverage:_ METHOD (locust + adversarial): (1) Read the required docs in full — cutover.md, modifier.md (esp. §6 "the unit plane — a self-accumulator" + §2/§2a), cascade-engine-430.md §4 "Modifier" (the "exactly 91 setters, CvUnit.cpp:11386…30937" line), and legacy-value-calc-map.md §6/§11.4 (unit-plane, unitInput) + §12 (unit-plane dump ENTIRELY ABSENT). (2) Enumerated the setter stack MECHANICALLY: `grep '::changeExtra' Sources/Engine/CvUnit.cpp` returns EXACTLY 91 definitions (count-verified = 91), matching the demolition-map claim bit-for-bit. All 91 are listed with file:line in the cutSites groups below (I grouped them; the group `locations` carry representative anchors + I state the full member list per group in notes so none is dropped by judgment). (3) Recursed each setter → its `m_iExtra*` storage field (CvUnit.h, grepped: scalar `m_iExtra*` block 1564-1693, keyed maps `m_*KeyedInfo` 1752-1756, pointer arrays `m_aiExtra*` 1738-1744, plus the inner keyed structs' `m_iExtra*` at 242-314) → its `getExtra*` reader (verified the change/field/get triad on representatives: ExtraStrength 11383/11388, ExtraWithdrawal 15597/15602, ExtraCombatPercent 16248/16255, ExtraAttackCombatModifier 15606-15625 with the commander/commodore re-walk). (4) Traced the APPLY-LOOPS that FEED the accumulators: `processPromotion` (CvUnit.cpp:18678) folds CvPromotionInfo `get*Change` × iChange into the stack (18798-18875); `processUnitCombat` (CvUnit.cpp:18283) folds CvUnitCombatInfo (18316-18395); `processLoadedSpecialUnit` (28851) folds CvSpecialUnitInfo (28856-28857). (5) Verified SERIALIZATION: unlike the CvCity yield accumulators (recompute-only/derived), the unit `m_iExtra*` fields ARE name-tag serialized (WRAPPER_READ ~19445+, WRAPPER_WRITE ~20456/20545) — so deleting them is a @SAVEBREAK, a material cut fact. (6) CASCADE side: globbed Sources/Cascade/*.h — there is NO unit-plane modifier machine (YieldRate/CommerceCalc/BuildingPackage/PercentStack are CITY-scope only); CvJsonUnitInfo/CvJsonPromotionInfo/CvJsonUnitCombatInfo map the DATA (deposit lists via base CvJsonInfo + skills) but NO runtime self-accumulator consumer reads them. (7) STONEBASE: grepped /c/code/s2s/StoneBase/src — models Calc (yields/commerce/specialist) + CascadingEnabler (trainable-units = ENABLER not modifier) + FreeSpecialistAmount; ZERO unit extra-stat MODIFIER modeling. So the ENTIRE unit plane is NOT StoneBase-covered → Gate 1 for this channel is judged-vs-LEGACY, not vs StoneBase. EXCLUDED deliberately: the 6 `setExtra*` absolute-setters (setExtraStrengthModifier/setExtraCargoVolume/setExtraCombatModifierPerSize/Volume{More,Less}) — these are NOT part of the 91 changeExtra accumulator setters and are twin absolute-writers of the same fields; noted under uncertainties as a recurse-completeness item, not omitted silently. NOTE: line numbers drift; every anchor is cited by function name + line as read on this branch (json-data-migration).

#### DELETE: tally count-loops + demographics/score

_Coverage:_ Enumeration method: (1) Read tally.md + cutover.md + cascade-engine-430.md §4 Tally in full to fix the channel scope (getBuildingPrereqBuilding loops + demographics/score + other cross-scope count re-scans). (2) Read the cascade replacement surface end-to-end: CvCascadeTally.{h,cpp} (buildingCount/unitCount(entity,type,scope) — the accessor) and CvCascadeBuildingCascade.cpp (the prereq-amount loop + scaledPrereq + capped + specialCount — the live tally-reads). (3) Grepped every getBuildingPrereqBuilding call site (5 engine/UI/diag + 3 CvCityAI) and read each. (4) Grepped getPrereqNumOfBuildings / PrereqNum loops. (5) Traced the calculateScore + getPopScore/getLandScore/getWondersScore/getTechScore bodies — found they are O(1) member reads / Python delegation, NOT count re-scans (the §4 map over-claims them). (6) Adversarial extension per 'ALL means ALL': hunted the allowed-cap surface (isBuildingMaxedOut / isBuildingGroupMaxedOut / getBuildingGroupCount / isUnitMaxedOut on CvPlayer/CvTeam/CvGame) — all O(1) reads of object-owned aggregates (enabler-cap, keep for tally axis); found countNumBuildings (a DISTINCT cities-having re-scan, semantically ≠ getBuildingCount); and the higher-scope HAS re-scans CvTeam::getHasReligionCount/getHasCorporationCount (team roll-up loops, tally-shaped but domains not yet wired). (7) Confirmed against StoneBase (BuildingCascade.cs GroupCount/ScaledPrereq/BuildingCounts at lines 74/85-87/111-117/181/192) that the count-read is the mapped source, so Gate 1 is satisfied — the getBuildingPrereqBuilding count loops have cut sites in the cascade prereq-amount loop reading cascadeTally().buildingCount. Deliberately EXCLUDED as out-of-axis: the enabler gate FUNCTIONS themselves (canConstructInternal/canTrain deletion) belong to the Enabler channel; here only their tally-subsumed count-READS are line-itemed. The maxed-out/cap functions read object-owned O(1) counts and are the enabler's 'allowed' cap — marked keep for this axis to avoid double-claiming. Key correction surfaced vs the §4 demolition map: the score scans (calculateScore + get*Score) are NOT cross-object count re-scan loops in live code — calculateScore is a Python delegation and get*Score read O(1) member accumulators. They are marked verify (score out of cascade scope, owner ruling), not deleted. This should be reflected back into cascade-engine-430.md §4 Tally.

_Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +5 missed, 3 corrections.

<details><summary>adversarial method / proof-of-search</summary>

Ran independent greps across ALL of Sources/ for the legacy count families and cross-checked against the tally accessor surface and StoneBase. Searches: `getBuildingPrereqBuilding` (16 hits — all engine call sites + AI + UI + Http + Cascade port + Python wrappers accounted for), `countNumBuildings` (all callers), `getUnitCount`/`getUnitCountPlusMaking` (full family), the score family (`getPopScore`/`getLandScore`/`getWondersScore`/`getTechScore`/`calculateScore` + change*Score maintainers), `getPrereqNumOf*`, and the `is*MaxedOut` allowed-cap gates. VERIFIED-CORRECT in the finder: canConstructInternal loops #1 (CvPlayer.cpp:6717-6726) and #2 (:6782-6795); CvCity autobuild loop (:1502-1511); getBuildingPrereqBuilding scaling port (scaledPrereq); the CvCityAI building heuristics (6535/14214/14481); the score accessors are O(1) member reads + a vassal SCORE-roll-up loop (NOT a building/unit count re-scan) maintained incrementally via change*Score (changeWondersScore CvCity.cpp:5119, changeTechScore CvPlayer.cpp:30939) — so `verify`/no-cut is right; calculateScore is a pure Python delegation (:4435) — `verify`, out of cascade scope, correct. The tally exposes ONLY buildingCount + unitCount (CvCascadeTally.h:39-40; impl reads getBuildingCount/getUnitCount O(1) for EMPIRE, sums over tallyPlayerInScope for TEAM/WORLD). buildingCount reads m_paiBuildingCount (multi-instance) so it is NOT interchangeable with countNumBuildings (cities-having) — finder's `verify` on countNumBuildings is sound. RESIDUAL RISK / borderline (noted, not filed as missed): (a) the `is*MaxedOut` allowed-cap gates (CvGame.cpp:5091/5112, CvPlayer.cpp:13568/13821, CvTeam.cpp:4607) are the `allowed`-cap enforcement (tally.md §3.2) but read O(1) created/aggregate counts, NOT re-scan loops — out of this DELETE-loop channel, belong to the enabler `allowed`-cap channel. (b) CvGameTextMgr.cpp:17732-17744 is a cross-building re-scan loop but reads STATIC prereq data (getPrereqNumOfBuildings().getValue) not a live count — it is the #195 reverse-index territory, not a tally count-read. (c) The CvHttpServer /state count emitters (:950/:960/:3119) read the object aggregate as SHADOW ground-truth — routing them through cascadeTally() would make the shadow tautological, so likely KEEP. StoneBase cross-check: BuildingCascade.cs models only BuildingCounts (the m_paiBuildingCount aggregate) + ScaledPrereq — no unit-count, AI-value, or score sources — so the unit-side and AI heuristics have no StoneBase source (n/a), consistent with the finder.

</details>

### Axis: REWIRE

#### REWIRE: unit skills consumers

_Coverage:_ METHOD (exhaustive + adversarial per DEC-all-means-all): (1) Read skills.md §1+§2 IN FULL for the authoritative skill list; (2) read json.md §8 (the classification system + the 3a wiring directive) and cascade-engine-430 §4/§7 for the cut model; (3) extracted the EXACT legacy XML field→skill-name mapping from the two ground-truth curators — Tools/Migration/curate_promotion.py (CAP_BOOL/CAP_PAIR/CAP_COUNT/CAP_LIST/SKILL_UNITCOMBAT_LIST) and curate_unit.py (CAP_BOOL/CAP_COUNT/DCM_AIRBOMB + pass2 CAP_LIST) — so every skill is grounded in the curator that produces it; (4) traced each skill's ENGINE reader in Sources/{Engine,AI,UI} via grep for the accessor, and READ the CvUnit getter bodies to CLASSIFY the cut mechanism into three patterns: Class A = count-fed (CvUnit::isX() reads m_iXCount; rewire the processUnitCombat/processPromotion FEED reading kUnitCombat.isX()/kPromotion.isX() → CvJsonInfo.skills.X; the accumulator + all combat/movement consumers stay cascade-populated), Class B = intrinsic getter (CvUnit::isX() reads m_pUnitInfo->isX() directly; rewire the single getter → unit's own CvJsonInfo.skills.X), HYBRID = both (m_iXCount + m_pUnitInfo->isX(); rewire feed AND getter). Confirmed the two FEED blocks: processUnitCombat CvUnit.cpp ~:18420-:18490, processPromotion ~:18745-:18988. Confirmed the AI direct-Info value evaluators in CvPlayerAI.cpp (~:27051-:33074) reading kPromotion.isX()/kUnitCombat.isX() also rewire to the source's CvJsonInfo.skills.X. CUT MODEL (per json §8 item 3a): skills.X is mapped onto the game object as the new skills array; each engine SYSTEM reads skills.X exactly as it read the legacy per-flag. The many downstream pUnit->isX() consumers (combat/movement/pathing) are NOT individually rewired — they read the CvUnit runtime state which the cascade now populates; the rewire is at the FEED (Class A) or the single intrinsic getter (Class B). EXHAUSTIVE over skills.md: enumerated ALL §1 validated skills, ALL §1 per-type-keyed skills, ALL §2 engine-grounded skills, the §4 grant/revoke pairs (stampede/attackOnlyCities/ignoreNoEntryLevel/ignoreZoneOfControl/fliesToMove — each an Add/Subtract count feed), the DEAD items (trap* → delete; triggerBeforeAttack → delete; paralyze → delete-and-migrate-to-state; oneUp → verify), and the RE-CLASSIFIED items (mechanized → tags channel; stateReligion → enabler requires.build; military* → tags — flagged for their proper channel, not silently dropped). DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED (belong to sibling channels, flagged not dropped): the unit STAT modifier families (strength/withdrawal/firstStrike/bombard/collateral/air/heal/movement/experience/workRate/cargo/vision/capture etc. — MODIFIER channel, curate_*.py FAMILIES/STRENGTH tables), unit tags (military/civilian/worker/spy/gunpowder/mechanized/outlaw — TAGS channel), the vision/LOS resolver (invisible/seeInvisible/visibilityIntensity — a non-cascade vision channel), and grants (foundBuildings/greatPersonAction/promotions — GRANTS channel). Building/Feature isNukeImmune and Terrain isFound explicitly noted as OTHER channels to avoid a wrong cross-cut.

_Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +14 missed, 0 corrections.

#### REWIRE: unit tags + IS_<TAG> + military counting

_Coverage:_ Enumerated exhaustively by grepping every reference to the three military* flags (isMilitarySupport/isMilitaryHappiness/isMilitaryProduction + the isMilitaryBranch alias), the spy (isSpy/m_bSpy/PUF_isSpy), and criminal/outlaw (isCriminal/PUF_isCriminal/UNITCOMBAT_CRIMINAL/UNITAI_INFILTRATOR) surfaces across Sources/ and Assets/Python, then traced each getter to its accumulator + every consumer. Followed each military* flag from its CvUnitInfo member+readXml+getter, through the CvUnit engine alias, to the count/pool accumulators (m_iNumMilitaryUnits, m_iUnitUpkeepMilitary100, m_iMilitaryHappinessUnits) and every read site, plus the PUF_* plot-count predicates (the AI counting surface) and the CyInfoInterface1 Python export + its RevolutionWatchAdvisor.py consumer. Verified the CASCADE side: tags map into CvJsonUnitInfo::tags (CvJsonUnitInfo.cpp:17) but there is NO IS_<TAG> predicate (CvCascadeCondition.h CASC_PRED_* enumerated in full) and NO tag-counting consumer — so every replacement is 'NONE FOUND (unbuilt)', consistent with cascade-engine-430.md §3a. Confirmed via the StoneBase sibling repo that it never modelled unit tags/military (only unrelated modifier 'tier tags'), so this channel contributes NO Gate-1 StoneBase-mapped source — it is a pure Gate-3 classification-consumption rewire, blocked on the unbuilt predicate surface. DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED: (1) the ~60 UNITAI_INFILTRATOR sites in CvPlayerAI/CvUnitAI/CvTeamAI/CvSelectionGroupAI — these are AI-ROLE dispatch (switch on eUnitAI), not a criminal-classification gate, so out of the tag axis; (2) CvCityAI AI_isMilitaryProductionCity/m_bMilitaryProductionCity — a per-city AI designation cache, NOT driven by the unit militaryProduction flag; (3) gunpowder/mechanized/mounted — explicitly deferred (post-migration unitcombat pass).</coverageNotes> <parameter name="uncertainties">["The outlaw/criminal SIGNAL diverges three ways and I could not reconcile which is authoritative for the cut: tags.md says outlaw = UNITCOMBAT_CRIMINAL primary-or-subcombat (22 units), the spec's 'old gate' was UNITAI_INFILTRATOR (13 units), but the LIVE engine isCriminal() actually uses INSIDIOUSNESS (getInsidiousnessTotal(true)>0), which matches neither. Rewiring isCriminal()/PUF_isCriminal to the combat-class outlaw tag is a behaviour change vs the live insidiousness gate — needs owner/spec confirmation (do insidiousness-carrying units without UNITCOMBAT_CRIMINAL lose criminal status?).","PUF_isMissionary is capability-based (canSpread at runtime), not a static type membership; whether the 'missionary' tag (from UNITAI_MISSIONARY) should replace this runtime check, or whether they are intentionally different concepts, is unresolved in the specs I read.","civilian/worker/settler/merchant have NO discrete legacy classification getter to point a cut site at — the classification is implicit in scattered UNITAI role checks. A per-UNITAI-role audit is needed to enumerate every rewire site; I could not exhaustively map those to individual cut sites from the tags channel alone (they belong to a broader UNITAI-vs-tags analysis).","The bSpy-skill-vs-spy-tag reconciliation is flagged OPEN in tags.md (§Open: 'drop the skill') but not yet decided/implemented; the exact cut (drop CAP entry from curate_promotion.py/curate_unit.py vs keep) is a spec-pending item, not groundable from live code.","I did not verify whether removing the CvUnitInfo military* / bSpy members is save-compatible; they are checksummed (CvUnitInfo.cpp:2945-2949) which affects XML checksum only, but any @SAVEBREAK implication for units carrying these was not traced."]

_Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +8 missed, 4 corrections.

<details><summary>adversarial method / proof-of-search</summary>

Ran INDEPENDENT greps across all of Sources/ for the whole family: isMilitaryHappiness|isMilitaryProduction|isMilitarySupport|isMilitaryBranch (44 hits), the PUF surface (PUF_isMilitaryHappiness/isSpy/isMissionary/isCriminal/isWantedCriminal), isSpy|isCriminal (100+ hits), getMilitaryHappiness*/getHappyPerMilitaryUnit, getUnitUpkeepMilitary*/getUnitUpkeepNet/getFinalUnitUpkeepChange, and TagTypes/isTag/TAG_SPY/TAG_HIDDEN_NATIONALITY. Confirmed against the cascade side (CvCascadeCondition.h:36-53 CvCascPredKind enum) that NO IS_MILITARY/IS_SPY/IS_OUTLAW/IS_MISSIONARY/tag predicate exists yet — the finder's uniform 'cascadeReplacement = NONE FOUND' is CORRECT. Confirmed the demolition doc §4 (cascade-engine-430.md:171-172) names the BoolExpr Is(tag)/TagTypes registry as the DETOUR that CvCascadeCondition replaces — the finder never mapped that mechanism. StoneBase: re-confirmed it is a city-yield/commerce dry-calc and never modelled unit tags/military/spy — 'n/a' correct for every site. Finder's list was an explicit SAMPLE (wrote 'sample'/'reads' for spy & upkeep) and is materially incomplete: missed the second spy flag (CvUnitCombatInfo::isSpy), the unit-combat isForMilitary classification, the legacy TagTypes predicate seam, the military-count resync loop, ~10 IS_MILITARY upkeep/count consumer sites, and one serialization error. Residual risk: the ~100 raw isSpy() reads all resolve through the single CvUnitInfo::isSpy getter (rewiring the getter to the spy tag covers them uniformly), so I enumerated DISTINCT mechanisms, not all ~130 leaf call-lines.

#### REWIRE: empire capabilities (team abilities)

_Coverage:_ ENUMERATION METHOD (locust, exhaustive): (1) Read CvTeam.h in full and mechanically listed EVERY is*/can*-with-backing-count ability member (the m_i*Count ints + the per-index m_paiTerrainTradeCount / m_aiCommerceFlexibleCount) = 22 distinct capability flags. (2) Confirmed the SET path for all is via CvTeam::processTech (CvTeam.cpp:5907) by grepping every change*Count call across all .cpp — the ONLY set sites are in processTech (tech-granted) plus the Cy* Python wrappers (not new grantors); sole exception is the player-level commerce-flexible count set by a BUILDING at CvPlayer.cpp:7478 (flagged as a divergence). (3) Grepped every is<Flag>() reader across Sources/ (full-tree then Engine-scoped) and attributed each consumer file:line to behaviour (founding/movement/trade-network/irrigation/AI-tech-value/help-text/Python). <details><summary>adversarial method / proof-of-search</summary>

Method: (1) Read capabilities.md + json.md §8 + cutover.md + cascade-engine-430.md §4 IN FULL. json.md §8 explicitly names the commerce-slider capabilities (setScienceRate/setCultureRate/setEspionageRate) which the finder OMITTED entirely. (2) Enumerated the DEFINITIVE tech-granted flag list from CvTeam::processTech (CvTeam.cpp:5907-6073) — this is the authoritative set of every monotonic tech-unlocked team capability. (3) Grep-confirmed every flag's getter, set-site, and DLL+Python consumers across ALL of Sources/. (4) Grounded EVERY cascade key name against the curator mapping Tools/Migration/mapping/TechInfo.json + curate_building.py — NOT invented. The finder covered only 10 of ~23 flags and left several key names as 'TBD' that are in fact grounded. The finder MISSED an entire family of tech-granted capability flags (all set in the same processTech block it already read for canFoundOnPeaks): the whole trading family beyond a truncated techTrading entry (goldTrading, mapTrading, openBordersTrading, limitedBordersTrading, defensivePactTrading, permanentAllianceTrading, vassalTrading, embassyTrading), plus extraWaterSeeFrom, desertFarming (isCanFarmDesert), rebaseAnywhere, and MOST IMPORTANTLY the CommerceFlexible slider capabilities (json.md §8's named setScienceRate/setCultureRate/setEspionageRate, dual-grantor tech+building). These have real DLL consumers (trade-diplomacy in CvPlayer::canTradeItem/getTradeDenial, AI valuation in CvPlayerAI/CvTeamAI, plot vision/farming validity, unit rebase, commerce slider gates) — deleting the legacy flags without rewiring them would silently break diplomacy, AI tech valuation, sliders, and vision. Residual risk / not fully chased: (a) mapCentering/mapVisible/language (TechInfo.json:38/39/41) are also kind:enabler tech channels routed to the capabilities block — mapCentering sets a per-PLAYER map flag (setMapCentering) not a monotonic team count, so it may be a special case; language likewise. These need a curator/spec confirmation of whether they emit into capabilities and whether they are true empire capabilities or player-map state. (b) DCM air-bomb (dcmAirBomb1/2) IS emitted as a capability (readJson survey found dcmAirBomb2 on techs) — a team combat capability, out of the strict is*/can* ability-flag naming but a genuine capabilities-block consumer. (c) I did NOT re-verify the finder's TerrainTrade blocker but confirmed the per-terrain shape is real (curator kind:enabler LIST). Recommend a follow-up minion sweep on mapCentering/mapVisible/language/dcmAirBomb to close the tail.

#### REWIRE: building attributes (CvCity flags)

_Coverage:_ Enumeration method (locust): read EVERY is*() bool getter in Sources/Infos/CvBuildingInfo.h (lines 32-71) mechanically — 40 building bool getters total. Cross-referenced each against the authoritative curator classification (Tools/Migration/curate_building.py CAP_ATTRIBUTES=17 held city-scope intrinsics, CAP_IDENTITY, REQUIRES_TAGS, STORE_TAGS, PASS2_TAGS, DROP_DEAD) to determine which land in the `attributes` block vs other cascade sections. For each of the 17 `attributes` flags I grepped Sources for the getter to find the live CvCity/CvPlayer/CvArea/CvTeam apply-loop (processBuilding/setHasBuilding count accumulation) and downstream consumer sites, and read the CvCity::processBuilding apply block (Engine/CvCity.cpp:4650-4900) + setHasBuilding (:14740-14780) to confirm the accumulate-then-read mechanism. Confirmed the cascade side: only attributes.governmentCenter is currently mapped onto CvJsonBuildingInfo (CvJsonBuildingInfo.cpp:22-28); the other 16 attributes are curator-EMITTED but parsed-but-skipped at the CvJsonBuildingInfo level (top-level `attributes` key is recognized in CvCascadeJsonParse.cpp:57 but only governmentCenter is read). Gate 1 (StoneBase) cross-check: grepped StoneBase — the ONLY building attribute it modelled is GovernmentCenter (BuildingInfo.cs:16 + PredicateKind.IsGovernmentCenter ConditionEvaluator.cs:229), which IS present in the cascade, so NO StoneBase-mapped source is missing for this channel. Confirmed borderObstacle/areaBorderObstacle are ONE flag (XML bBorderObstacle -> engine m_bAreaBorderObstacle, CvBuildingInfo.cpp:1733). DELIBERATELY cross-listed (not attributes, but shown to prove building-bool completeness): commerceFlexible->capabilities, noHolyCity->requires, forceTeamVoteEligible->enables.votes, damageAllAttackers->defense modifier, applyFreePromotionOnMove->DROPPED, noEnemyPillagingIncome->DEAD, and the requires/identity-bound bools (power/water/river/etc). Cut action = rewire for the 16 unmapped attributes (their JSON exists but needs a CvJsonBuildingInfo field + the engine consumer rewired to read it); governmentCenter = rewire (predicate live but engine count still legacy-driven).

_Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +7 missed, 2 corrections.

Cross-checked each against the finder's list: finder covered governmentCenter, nukeImmune, providesFreshWater, areaBorderObstacle(=borderObstacle), zoneOfControl, protectedCulture, noUnhappiness, noUnhealthyPopulation, buildingOnlyHealthy, forceAllTradeRoutes, and STARTED quarantine (its JSON was truncated mid-entry at 'loca'). MISSED entirely: mapCentering, teamShare, orbital, orbitalInfrastructure, capital, destroyedOnCapture. Verified via Grep of is*() family across all Sources/ (200 hits) + Read of every apply/consumer site. Confirmed cascade side: CvJsonBuildingInfo::mapFrom (Cascade/CvJsonBuildingInfo.cpp:23-28) parses the `attributes` block but reads ONLY governmentCenter (line 27) — every other attribute is NONE FOUND (parsed-but-skipped / not even referenced). Did NOT re-verify every downstream isCapital() consumer (dozens) since the apply-loop (setCapitalCity) is the single cut point; consumers all read via CvCity::isCapital() (getCapitalCity()==this).

<details><summary>adversarial method / proof-of-search</summary>

Read cutover.md, json.md §9 (policies block), and both curators (curate_civic.py POLICIES=17 flags, curate_trait.py POLICIES=10 flags + FREE_SPEC_PER_WONDER effects). Cross-checked every bool getter in Infos/CvCivicInfo.h (20 policy bools: 103-122) and Infos/CvTraitInfo.h (10 policy bools) against the curator dicts and against processCivics (starts CvPlayer.cpp:18005) / processTrait (starts 28463) accumulation sites. Confirmed only CvCivicInfo + CvTraitInfo carry these bools (no other Info type). The finder covered all 17 CIVIC policies but its JSON truncated after the FIRST trait row (nonStateReligionCommerce) — so ALL 9 remaining TRAIT policy accumulators were missed, plus 3 civic-only bools that the CIVIC curator does not emit (Gate 1 gaps), plus the pinpointed inquisition + allReligionsBanned accumulation lines the finder said it could not locate. Residual risk: the FreeSpecialistper{World,National,Team}Wonder trait bools (CvPlayer.cpp:28606-28608) accumulate changeFreeSpecialistper*Count and are reclassified by json.md §9 to a freeSpecialists MODIFIER (not policies) — out of THIS channel but a real cut concern the modifier channel must own; noted, not claimed here. isPolicy()/m_bPolicy (CvCivicInfo.cpp:662, consumed CvPlayer.cpp:8965) is a civic-swap ideology marker, a DIFFERENT field from the policy toggles — not a policies-block source.

</details>

### Axis: PREREQ

#### PREREQ: self-containment (cascade reads legacy-computed)

_Coverage:_ Method: read the design docs in full first — cascade-engine-430.md (Prerequisites/§4), cutover.md (Prerequisites: self-containment), DEC-calc-zero-ride-in (incl. the camouflaged-case clause), modifier-machine.md §2 (the enabler-active-state ruling), validation.md §pollution-guardrail. Then traced EVERY .cpp in Sources/Cascade/ for live-engine reads via a targeted grep (hasTrait/getStateReligion/isActiveBuilding/isDisabledBuilding/hasVicinityBonus/getYieldRate/getCommerceRate/isActiveCorporation/getBuildingCommerce/isGovernmentCenter/isConnectedTo/getModifiedIntValue/getExtraYield) plus full reads of the calc surface: CvCascadeConditionEval, MMKernel, PercentStack, YieldBasePackages, BuildingPackage, YieldRate, CommerceCalc, EnablerKernel, BuildingCascade, UnitCascade, CvCascadeEnabler (shadow), CvCascadeModifierMath (shadow harness). For each read I resolved the ENGINE backing (read CvCity.cpp isActiveCorporation/isGovernmentCenter/isPower, CvTeam.cpp getCorporationRevenueModifier, CvPlayer.cpp hasTrait/m_pabHasTrait) to classify RAW-saved-state vs COMPUTED-accumulator/gate. Deliberately EXCLUDED from cut sites: pure InfoRepo/CvJsonInfo reads and Info-CONFIG reads (getObsoleteTech, getSpecialBuilding, getMaxPlayerInstances, getBuildingPrereqModifier, getPrereqNumOfBuildings, isForceNoPrereqScaling, getYieldInfo constants) — these are static config, not legacy-COMPUTED-runtime outputs, so they are a Gate-3/data-mapping concern (JSON must carry them), NOT a self-containment ride-in on this axis; noted here so the exclusion is explicit. The comparison-boundary legacy reads in the two shadow harnesses are the sanctioned allowed use and are listed as a 'verify' seam, not a cut. Distinguished the 4 genuine/suspected COMPUTED ride-ins (trait membership; corporation-revenue-modifier accumulator; isActiveCorporation obsolete-tech gate; gov-center + power building-attribute accumulators) from the accepted CONNECTED state read (owner-sanctioned keep) and the full RAW input set (keep). Every file:line was read from live code this session.

_Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +4 missed, 1 corrections.

PART 1 (apply-loop): enumerated EVERY row of the grants-machine.md inventory table and traced each legacy apply-site to live code -- unit on-create promotions (CvUnit::init:367->doSetFreePromotions), settler foundBuildings (CvPlayer::found:6289), building repeatable spawn (doPropertyUnitSpawn:23829) + heal (doHeal:21761), building freePromotions (doPromotion:20677), building one-shot bFirst freeTechs/population/goldenAge (setupBuilding:14721-14819), the three TRAIT recurring/pulse grants (freePromotions:28715, eraAdvanceFreeSpecialist:12459, goldenAgeOnBirthOfGreatPerson:20482), tech first-discover (CvTeam::setHasTech:5451-5498), religion founder (foundReligion:8848), civic revolution (Python-only, verified no DLL apply), and the whole game-start set (initFreeState startingGold:1864 + civics:481/:1536 + CvGame::initFreeState free-techs:1212). Confirmed every already-wired DOMAIN emit (CASCADE_EVT_* at the emit sites). All Part-1 rows are cutAction=BLOCKED (apply-loop increment 5 not built) per the channel directive. Grounded the two-driver anchoring in CvCity::doTurn (:1330/:1340/:1385). PART 2 (blocked tail): enumerated every blocked item in data-migration-remaining.md (paralyze/state, unitcombat->tags, NPC civs/stronglyRestricted, corp-system, ranked-target/largestCity, missions/CvOutcome, revolution, random-events, leaderhead remap) and grounded each legacy mechanism + the consumer its cut blocks in live code. StoneBase confirmed to NOT model grants (it is a yield/commerce/enabler dry-calc; grep hits were incidental substrings) -> all grants stoneBaseSource=n/a, consistent with cutover.md Gate 1 (grants judged vs legacy engine, not StoneBase). Deliberately EXCLUDED: the trait simple/complex ENGINE swap (CvInfoReplacements, cascade-engine-430.md §6) -- it is an in-scope cut but belongs to a different (trait/modifier) channel, noted only to disambiguate from leaderhead remap; and the modifier accumulators (m_iNumUnitFullHeal is a processBuilding accumulator but its APPLY is a grant, so mapped here on the apply axis).

_Adversarial verdict:_ complete=**False**; +9 missed, 4 corrections.

KEY FINDING: the finder mapped increment-3c's TRIGGER emit (CvPlayer::initFreeUnits:1884 CASCADE_EVT_PLAYER_INIT) but did NOT enumerate the actual legacy game-start APPLY sites, which are spread across 5 functions in 3 files (CvPlayer::initFreeState, CvPlayer::initFreeUnits body, CvGame::initFreeState, CvCity::init, CvPlayer::initMore) — a DEC-all-means-all violation (the apply sites ARE the cut-sites; 'spread across init sites' is not an enumeration).
